Windmill Family Campground
Activities
Experience everything
Heated Pool
Swim in our newly renovated swimming pools! Opens around Father’s Day weather pending.
Mini Golf
Play a little putt-putt on our 9 hole mini golf course.
Hayrides
Hop on one of our hayrides! Pulled by an antique tracker that gives a relaxing ride throughout the park on Fridays and Saturdays!.
View More Activities
Home
Take a virtual tour
We created a virtual tour of the office, convenience store, games room, and the pool area.
Enjoy full 360 degree photos where you can “walk” through various rooms as well as view outside of our office and store.
GO TO VIRTUAL TOUR

Reviews
Best campground in the world i love this family campground
Lindsey Gray
Had a great 4 days,would have topped it off Rick,if could have had a picture of Joanne in Big Bird. Great catching up,see you again soon.
Derek Robinson
We stayed in one of the cabins and they were so much better than we expected. The cabin was so clean in great condition. My kids loved the bouncing bubble and the parks. We had a great time!
Julia Bursey
Very nice place to stay and the owner is very helpful, also great neighbors to lend a helping hand. Went out of their way to make our visit enjoyable
Steve Cameron
I loved my stay here. It’s a very well kept campground with huge, spacious sites - whether you like canopy cover or open, it’s fantastic.
The service staff are so friendly and accommodating... it was really a great experience.  I’d just like to really emphasize that point.
Jonathan Lees
Windmil Family Campground
2778 Perth County Road 163 Fullarton, Ontario, N0K 1H0

Windmill Family Campground
Activities
Experience everything
Heated Pool
Swim in our newly renovated swimming pools! Opens around Father’s Day weather pending.
Mini Golf
Play a little putt-putt on our 9 hole mini golf course.
Hayrides
Hop on one of our hayrides! Pulled by an antique tracker that gives a relaxing ride throughout the park on Fridays and Saturdays!.
View More Activities
Home
Take a virtual tour
We created a virtual tour of the office, convenience store, games room, and the pool area.
Enjoy full 360 degree photos where you can “walk” through various rooms as well as view outside of our office and store.

Reviews
Best campground in the world i love this family campground
Lindsey Gray
Had a great 4 days,would have topped it off Rick,if could have had a picture of Joanne in Big Bird. Great catching up,see you again soon.
Derek Robinson
We stayed in one of the cabins and they were so much better than we expected. The cabin was so clean in great condition. My kids loved the bouncing bubble and the parks. We had a great time!
Julia Bursey
Very nice place to stay and the owner is very helpful, also great neighbors to lend a helping hand. Went out of their way to make our visit enjoyable
Steve Cameron
I loved my stay here. It’s a very well kept campground with huge, spacious sites - whether you like canopy cover or open, it’s fantastic.
The service staff are so friendly and accommodating... it was really a great experience.  I’d just like to really emphasize that point.
Jonathan Lees
